####################
# Hitler Suicide
event = {
    id = 2003183
    random = no
    country = GER
    
    name = 2003183name
    desc = 2003183desc
    style = 2
    picture = "Hitler_Death"

    trigger = {
		incabinet = 1 # Hitler
		OR = {
			control = { province = 300  data = -2 } # W Berlin
			control = { province = 7266 data = -2 } # E Berlin
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = Germany country = -2 value = 80 }
			#Hitler is feeling more depressed than usual in late April around his birthday
			AND = {
				region = { region = Germany country = -2 value = 60 }
				month = April
				OR = {
					day = 20
					month = May
				}
				NOT = { month = June }
			}
		}
    }

    date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1933 }
    offset = 10
    deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1999 }

    action = {
        ai_chance = 1
        name = 2003183a
        command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
        command = { type = headofstate which = 11 } # Gцring
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 275 } # Gцbbels
		command = { type = chiefofarmy which = 285 } #Dцnitz
		command = { type = chiefofstaff which = 287 } #Schцrner
        command = { type = sleepminister which = 1 } # Hitler
    }
    action = {
        ai_chance = 3
        name = 2003183b
        command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
        command = { type = headofstate which = 12 } # Gцbbels
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 277 } # Himmler
		command = { type = chiefofarmy which = 285 } #Dцnitz
		command = { type = chiefofstaff which = 287 } #Schцrner
        command = { type = sleepminister which = 1 } # Hitler
    }
    action = {
        ai_chance = 6
        name = 2003183c
        command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
        command = { type = headofstate which = 276 } # Himmler
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 275 } # Gцbbels
		command = { type = chiefofarmy which = 285 } #Dцnitz
		command = { type = chiefofstaff which = 287 } #Schцrner
        command = { type = sleepminister which = 1 } # Hitler
    }
    action = {
        ai_chance = 90
        name = 2003183d
        command = { type = dissent value = 3 }
        command = { type = headofstate which = 13 } # Dцnitz
		command = { type = headofgovernment which = 30 } #Krosigk
		command = { type = chiefofarmy which = 285 } #Dцnitz
		command = { type = chiefofstaff which = 287 } #Schцrner
		#command = { type = armamentminister which = 64 } #Speer is fine too
        command = { type = sleepminister which = 1 } # Hitler
		command = { type = sleepminister which = 275 } # Gobbels also dies
    }
}

event = { 
	id = 2003215 
	random = no 
	country = SOV

	name = EVT_2003215_NAME
	desc = EVT_2003215_DESC
  	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK"
		trigger = { 
			OR = { 
				war = { country = USA country = JAP } 
				war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
			} 
			NOT = { 
				war = { country = JAP } 
				war = { country = ENG } 
				war = { country = USA } 
			}
		}
		
		command = { type = dissent value = 0.5 } #Foreign adventure, not a clear existential threat
		
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1 }
		
		command = { type = clrflag which = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		
		#Will give net +1 dissent if there was a non-aggression pact. Otherwise, no dissent.
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = SOV } } type = local_setflag which = JapSovNAP }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = JAP } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -4 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = belligerence which = SOV	value = 5  }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = belligerence which = JAP	value = -5 }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = local_clrflag which = JapSovNAP }
		
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = MAN country = SOV } } type = local_setflag which = JapSovNAP }
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = end_non_aggression which = SOV where = MAN } #will give 5 dissent
		command = { trigger = { local_flag = JapSovNAP } type = dissent value = -5 }

event = {
	id = 2049512
	random = no
	persistent = yes
	
	name = EVT_2049502_NAME #Joining the NAM
	desc = EVT_2049501_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"
	
	decision = {
		flag = NAMO
		NOT = { local_flag = NAMO }
		axis = 1
		NOT = {
			country = ISR
			country = U56
			AND = {
				continent = "Europe"
				NOT = { policy = { type = political_left value = 8 } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				country = IND
				country = INO
			}
			AND = {
				country = IND
				event = { id = 2001130 days = 720 } #At least two years from independence
			}
			AND = {
				country = INO
				event = { id = 2011028 days = 720 } #At least two years from independence
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			atwar = yes
			ispuppet = yes
			participant = { value = 1 }
			#participant = { value = 2 } 
			#participant = { value = 3 }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		flag = NAMO
		NOT = { local_flag = NAMO }
		axis = 1
		NOT = {
			country = ISR
			country = U56
			AND = {
				continent = "Europe"
				NOT = { policy = { type = political_left value = 8 } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				country = IND
				country = INO
			}
			AND = {
				country = IND
				event = { id = 2001130 days = 720 } #At least two years from independence
			}
			AND = {
				country = INO
				event = { id = 2011028 days = 720 } #At least two years from independence
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			atwar = yes
			ispuppet = yes
			participant = { value = 1 }
			AND = {
				participant = { value = 2 }
				NOT = { policy = { type = political_left value = 8 } }
			}
			participant = { value = 3 }
		}

			#Initial members were almost all either socialists or Arabs or Arab socialists.

			country = TUN
			country = ALG
			country = MOR
			country = SYR
			country = LEB
			country = JOR
		}
		NOT = { ideology = leninist }
		random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1944 }
	offset = 20
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

		name = EVT_2049501_ACTA #Join NAM
		ai_chance = 100
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { country = IND } type = alliance_leader where = 1 } #India automatically becomes leader if it joins
		
		command = { type = alliance where = 1 }

		command = { type = relation which = IND value = 100 }
		command = { type = relation which = U27 value = 100 }
		command = { type = relation which = EGY value = 100 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = NAMO }
	}
	action = {
		name = EVT_2049501_ACTB #Not interested (makes the decision go away)

		command = { type = local_setflag which = NAMO }
